# Every act in this repository

2317 acts, 2,320 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its kind, year and identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<identifier>/` (acts published before that layout keep their own folder, listed in data/paths.json); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [data/acts.json](data/acts.json).
